This guide covers the essential migration steps from Spring Boot 2.7.x to 3.2.x.\n\n## Key Changes\n\n### 1. Minimum Requirements\n- **Java Version**: Java 17 minimum (Java 21 recommended for latest features)\n- **Spring Framework**: 6.x (instead of 5.x)\n- **Hibernate**: 6.x (instead of 5.x)\n\n### 2. Dependency Version Updates\n\n#### Maven POM Changes\n```xml\n<parent>\n    <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>\n    <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-parent</artifactId>\n    <version>3.2.0</version> <!-- Updated from 2.7.x -->\n</parent>\n\n<properties>\n    <java.version>21</java.version> <!-- Updated from 1.8 or 11 -->\n</properties>\n```\n\n#### Key Dependency Changes\n- Spring Boot Starters: Automatically upgraded via parent POM\n- Spring Security: 6.x (major changes to configuration)\n- Spring Data JPA: Uses Hibernate 6.x\n- Validation: No changes to `spring-boot-starter-validation`\n\n### 3. Jakarta EE Migration (Critical)\nAll `javax.*` packages have been renamed to `jakarta.*`:\n\n**Before (Spring Boot 2.x):**\n```java\nimport javax.persistence.*;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.*;\nimport javax.servlet.*;\n```\n\n**After (Spring Boot 3.x):**\n```java\nimport jakarta.persistence.*;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.*;\nimport jakarta.servlet.*;\n```\n\n### 4. Common Dependency Updates\n\n#### JWT Libraries\nThe old `jjwt` 0.9.1 is not compatible. Upgrade to latest:\n\n```xml\n<!-- Old -->\n<dependency>\n    <groupId>io.jsonwebtoken</groupId>\n    <artifactId>jjwt</artifactId>\n    <version>0.9.1</version>\n</dependency>\n\n<!-- New - Use the modular approach -->\n<dependency>\n    <groupId>io.jsonwebtoken</groupId>\n    <artifactId>jjwt-api</artifactId>\n    <version>0.12.3</version>\n</dependency>\n<dependency>\n    <groupId>io.jsonwebtoken</groupId>\n    <artifactId>jjwt-impl</artifactId>\n    <version>0.12.3</version>\n    <scope>runtime</scope>\n</dependency>\n<dependency>\n    <groupId>io.jsonwebtoken</groupId>\n    <artifactId>jjwt-jackson</artifactId>\n    <version>0.12.3</version>\n    <scope>runtime</scope>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n### 5. Configuration Properties\n\nMost configuration properties remain the same, but some have changed:\n\n**Deprecated properties:**\n- `spring.jpa.hibernate.use-new-id-generator-mappings` (removed)\n- Some actuator endpoints paths changed\n\n**New recommended properties:**\n```properties\n# Spring Boot 3.x recommendations\nspring.jpa.open-in-view=false\nspring.jpa.show-sql=false\n```\n\n### 6. Removed Dependencies\n\nRemove JAXB dependencies if added for Java 8 compatibility:\n```xml\n<!-- Remove these - not needed in Java 17+ -->\n<dependency>\n    <groupId>javax.xml.bind</groupId>\n    <artifactId>jaxb-api</artifactId>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n### 7. Testing Updates\n\nTest dependencies are automatically updated via the parent POM:\n```xml\n<dependency>\n    <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>\n    <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-test</artifactId>\n    <scope>test</scope>\n</dependency>\n```\n\nJUnit 5 is now mandatory (JUnit 4 support removed).\n\n### 8. Common Migration Issues\n\n#### Issue: NoClassDefFoundError for javax.* classes\n**Solution**: Replace all `javax.*` imports with `jakarta.*`\n\n#### Issue: Security configuration compilation errors\n**Solution**: Migrate to component-based security (see Spring Security 6 migration guide)\n\n#### Issue: RestTemplate deprecation warnings\n**Solution**: Migrate to RestClient or WebClient\n\n#### Issue: Hibernate validation errors\n**Solution**: Review entity mappings for Hibernate 6 compatibility\n\n## Migration Checklist\n\n- [ ] Update Spring Boot parent version to 3.2.x\n- [ ] Update Java version to 17 or higher\n- [ ] Replace all `javax.*` imports with `jakarta.*`\n- [ ] Update third-party dependencies (JWT, etc.)\n- [ ] Remove Java 8 compatibility dependencies (JAXB)\n- [ ] Update Spring Security configuration\n- [ ] Migrate RestTemplate to RestClient\n- [ ] Test all endpoints and functionality\n- [ ] Run full test suite\n\n## References\n- [Spring Boot 3.0 Migration Guide](https://github.com/spring-projects/spring-boot/wiki/Spring-Boot-3.0-Migration-Guide)\n- [Spring Boot 3.2 Release Notes](https://github.com/spring-projects/spring-boot/wiki/Spring-Boot-3.2-Release-Notes)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":126,"cache_read_input_tokens":26664,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":126,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6f9f7172-e84f-4a4a-a2a4-a3ac0561d4b4","uuid":"76b8e484-30ff-40d1-9429-fe3c97b839df"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01XMiARMjFrkEVduz32UEpJg","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01JLMqVYBsM481yajuRfuAC6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/workspace/environment/skills/java-8-to-21-upgrade.md","content":"# Java 8 to Java 21 Upgrade Guide\n\n## Overview\nJava 21 is an LTS (Long-Term Support) release that includes features from Java 9 through Java 21. This guide covers the essential aspects of upgrading from Java 8 to Java 21.\n\n## Key Benefits\n\n### 1. Performance Improvements\n- **ZGC and G1GC enhancements**: Better garbage collection\n- **JIT compiler improvements**: Faster execution\n- **Startup time improvements**: Faster application startup\n\n### 2. Language Features\n\n#### Pattern Matching (Java 16+)\n```java\n// Old Java 8 way\nif (obj instanceof String) {\n    String str = (String) obj;\n    System.out.println(str.length());\n}\n\n// New Java 16+ way\nif (obj instanceof String str) {\n    System.out.println(str.length());\n}\n```\n\n#### Records (Java 14+)\n```java\n// Old Java 8 way - requires full class with getters, equals, hashCode\npublic class UserDTO {\n    private final String username;\n    private final String email;\n\n    public UserDTO(String username, String email) {\n        this.username = username;\n        this.email = email;\n    }\n    // ... getters, equals, hashCode, toString\n}\n\n// New Java 14+ way - automatic\npublic record UserDTO(String username, String email) {}\n```\n\n#### Text Blocks (Java 15+)\n```java\n// Old way\nString json = \"{\\n\" +\n              \"  \\\"name\\\": \\\"John\\\",\\n\" +\n              \"  \\\"age\\\": 30\\n\" +\n              \"}\";\n\n// New way\nString json = \"\"\"\n    {\n      \"name\": \"John\",\n      \"age\": 30\n    }\n    \"\"\";\n```\n\n#### Switch Expressions (Java 14+)\n```java\n// Old way\nString result;\nswitch (role) {\n    case ADMIN:\n        result = \"Administrator\";\n        break;\n    case USER:\n        result = \"Regular User\";\n        break;\n    default:\n        result = \"Unknown\";\n}\n\n// New way\nString result = switch (role) {\n    case ADMIN -> \"Administrator\";\n    case USER -> \"Regular User\";\n    default -> \"Unknown\";\n};\n```\n\n#### Sealed Classes (Java 17+)\n```java\npublic sealed interface Role permits Admin, User, Guest {\n    String getDisplayName();\n}\n\npublic final class Admin implements Role {\n    public String getDisplayName() { return \"Administrator\"; }\n}\n\npublic final class User implements Role {\n    public String getDisplayName() { return \"Regular User\"; }\n}\n\npublic final class Guest implements Role {\n    public String getDisplayName() { return \"Guest\"; }\n}\n```\n\n### 3. API Improvements\n\n#### String Methods\n```java\n// isBlank() - Java 11+\nString str = \"  \";\nboolean blank = str.isBlank(); // true\n\n// lines() - Java 11+\nString multiline = \"line1\\nline2\\nline3\";\nList<String> lines = multiline.lines().collect(Collectors.toList());\n\n// repeat() - Java 11+\nString repeated = \"abc\".repeat(3); // \"abcabcabc\"\n\n// strip(), stripLeading(), stripTrailing() - Java 11+\nString stripped = \"  text  \".strip(); // \"text\" (handles Unicode)\n```\n\n#### Collection Factory Methods (Java 9+)\n```java\n// Old way\nList<String> list = new ArrayList<>();\nlist.add(\"one\");\nlist.add(\"two\");\n\n// New way\nList<String> list = List.of(\"one\", \"two\");\nSet<String> set = Set.of(\"one\", \"two\");\nMap<String, Integer> map = Map.of(\"one\", 1, \"two\", 2);\n```\n\n#### Optional Enhancements\n```java\n// ifPresentOrElse() - Java 9+\noptional.ifPresentOrElse(\n    value -> System.out.println(value),\n    () -> System.out.println(\"Not present\")\n);\n\n// or() - Java 9+\nOptional<String> result = optional.or(() -> Optional.of(\"default\"));\n\n// stream() - Java 9+\nStream<String> stream = optional.stream();\n```\n\n#### Stream API Enhancements\n```java\n// takeWhile() and dropWhile() - Java 9+\nList<Integer> numbers = List.of(1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6);\nList<Integer> taken = numbers.stream()\n    .takeWhile(n -> n < 4)\n    .collect(Collectors.toList()); // [1, 2, 3]\n\n// iterate() with predicate - Java 9+\nStream.iterate(0, n -> n < 10, n -> n + 1)\n    .forEach(System.out::println);\n\n// toList() - Java 16+\nList<String> list = stream.toList(); // Shorter than .collect(Collectors.toList())\n```\n\n### 4. Module System (Java 9+)\n\nWhile not mandatory, you can use the module system for better encapsulation:\n\n```java\n// module-info.java\nmodule com.example.userservice {\n    requires spring.boot;\n    requires spring.web;\n    requires spring.data.jpa;\n\n    exports com.example.userservice.controller;\n    opens com.example.userservice.model to org.hibernate.orm.core;\n}\n```\n\n### 5. Maven Configuration\n\n#### Update Maven Compiler Plugin\n```xml\n<build>\n    <plugins>\n        <plugin>\n            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>\n            <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>\n            <version>3.11.0</version>\n            <configuration>\n                <source>21</source>\n                <target>21</target>\n                <release>21</release>\n            </configuration>\n        </plugin>\n    </plugins>\n</build>\n```\n\nOr simply in properties (when using Spring Boot parent):\n```xml\n<properties>\n    <java.version>21</java.version>\n</properties>\n```\n\n### 6. Removed Features\n\n#### Removed in Java 11+\n- Java EE modules (JAXB, JAX-WS, etc.) - use Jakarta EE instead\n- CORBA support\n- Java FX (separate download)\n\n#### Removed in Java 17+\n- Security Manager (deprecated for removal)\n- Applet API\n\n### 7. Virtual Threads (Java 21 Preview)\n\nJava 21 introduces virtual threads for improved concurrency:\n\n```java\n// Traditional thread\nThread thread = new Thread(() -> {\n    // task\n});\nthread.start();\n\n// Virtual thread - lightweight\nThread virtualThread = Thread.startVirtualThread(() -> {\n    // task\n});\n\n// With ExecutorService\ntry (var executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {\n    executor.submit(() -> {\n        // task\n    });\n}\n```\n\n### 8. Common Migration Issues\n\n#### Issue: Removed packages (javax.xml.bind, etc.)\n**Solution**: Already handled in Spring Boot 3.x which uses Jakarta EE\n\n#### Issue: Changed default garbage collector\n**Solution**: Test application performance; adjust GC settings if needed\n\n#### Issue: Stricter null handling\n**Solution**: Review code for potential NullPointerExceptions\n\n## Compatibility Notes\n\n- **Bytecode compatibility**: Java 21 can run code compiled with Java 8\n- **Source compatibility**: Most Java 8 code compiles with Java 21\n- **Binary compatibility**: JAR files from Java 8 work in Java 21\n- **Deprecated APIs**: Some APIs deprecated since Java 8 may be removed\n\n## Migration Checklist\n\n- [ ] Update `java.version` property to 21\n- [ ] Update IDE Java SDK to Java 21\n- [ ] Update CI/CD pipeline to use Java 21\n- [ ] Review and remove Java 8 workarounds\n- [ ] Consider using new Java 21 features where appropriate\n- [ ] Test application thoroughly\n- [ ] Update documentation\n\n## References\n- [Java 21 Release Notes](https://www.oracle.com/java/technologies/javase/21-relnotes.html)\n- [Java Version History](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Java_version_history)\n- [Migration Guide to Java 17](https://docs.oracle.com/en/java/javase/17/migrate/getting-started.html)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1441,"cache_read_input_tokens":26790,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1441,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6f9f7172-e84f-4a4a-a2a4-a3ac0561d4b4","uuid":"bd5055fd-3d5e-4147-823c-4bcbd9070b2d"}

This guide covers the essential aspects of upgrading from Java 8 to Java 21.\n\n## Key Benefits\n\n### 1. Performance Improvements\n- **ZGC and G1GC enhancements**: Better garbage collection\n- **JIT compiler improvements**: Faster execution\n- **Startup time improvements**: Faster application startup\n\n### 2. Language Features\n\n#### Pattern Matching (Java 16+)\n```java\n// Old Java 8 way\nif (obj instanceof String) {\n    String str = (String) obj;\n    System.out.println(str.length());\n}\n\n// New Java 16+ way\nif (obj instanceof String str) {\n    System.out.println(str.length());\n}\n```\n\n#### Records (Java 14+)\n```java\n// Old Java 8 way - requires full class with getters, equals, hashCode\npublic class UserDTO {\n    private final String username;\n    private final String email;\n\n    public UserDTO(String username, String email) {\n        this.username = username;\n        this.email = email;\n    }\n    // ... getters, equals, hashCode, toString\n}\n\n// New Java 14+ way - automatic\npublic record UserDTO(String username, String email) {}\n```\n\n#### Text Blocks (Java 15+)\n```java\n// Old way\nString json = \"{\\n\" +\n              \"  \\\"name\\\": \\\"John\\\",\\n\" +\n              \"  \\\"age\\\": 30\\n\" +\n              \"}\";\n\n// New way\nString json = \"\"\"\n    {\n      \"name\": \"John\",\n      \"age\": 30\n    }\n    \"\"\";\n```\n\n#### Switch Expressions (Java 14+)\n```java\n// Old way\nString result;\nswitch (role) {\n    case ADMIN:\n        result = \"Administrator\";\n        break;\n    case USER:\n        result = \"Regular User\";\n        break;\n    default:\n        result = \"Unknown\";\n}\n\n// New way\nString result = switch (role) {\n    case ADMIN -> \"Administrator\";\n    case USER -> \"Regular User\";\n    default -> \"Unknown\";\n};\n```\n\n#### Sealed Classes (Java 17+)\n```java\npublic sealed interface Role permits Admin, User, Guest {\n    String getDisplayName();\n}\n\npublic final class Admin implements Role {\n    public String getDisplayName() { return \"Administrator\"; }\n}\n\npublic final class User implements Role {\n    public String getDisplayName() { return \"Regular User\"; }\n}\n\npublic final class Guest implements Role {\n    public String getDisplayName() { return \"Guest\"; }\n}\n```\n\n### 3. API Improvements\n\n#### String Methods\n```java\n// isBlank() - Java 11+\nString str = \"  \";\nboolean blank = str.isBlank(); // true\n\n// lines() - Java 11+\nString multiline = \"line1\\nline2\\nline3\";\nList<String> lines = multiline.lines().collect(Collectors.toList());\n\n// repeat() - Java 11+\nString repeated = \"abc\".repeat(3); // \"abcabcabc\"\n\n// strip(), stripLeading(), stripTrailing() - Java 11+\nString stripped = \"  text  \".strip(); // \"text\" (handles Unicode)\n```\n\n#### Collection Factory Methods (Java 9+)\n```java\n// Old way\nList<String> list = new ArrayList<>();\nlist.add(\"one\");\nlist.add(\"two\");\n\n// New way\nList<String> list = List.of(\"one\", \"two\");\nSet<String> set = Set.of(\"one\", \"two\");\nMap<String, Integer> map = Map.of(\"one\", 1, \"two\", 2);\n```\n\n#### Optional Enhancements\n```java\n// ifPresentOrElse() - Java 9+\noptional.ifPresentOrElse(\n    value -> System.out.println(value),\n    () -> System.out.println(\"Not present\")\n);\n\n// or() - Java 9+\nOptional<String> result = optional.or(() -> Optional.of(\"default\"));\n\n// stream() - Java 9+\nStream<String> stream = optional.stream();\n```\n\n#### Stream API Enhancements\n```java\n// takeWhile() and dropWhile() - Java 9+\nList<Integer> numbers = List.of(1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6);\nList<Integer> taken = numbers.stream()\n    .takeWhile(n -> n < 4)\n    .collect(Collectors.toList()); // [1, 2, 3]\n\n// iterate() with predicate - Java 9+\nStream.iterate(0, n -> n < 10, n -> n + 1)\n    .forEach(System.out::println);\n\n// toList() - Java 16+\nList<String> list = stream.toList(); // Shorter than .collect(Collectors.toList())\n```\n\n### 4. This is one of the most significant changes when migrating to Spring Boot 3.x.\n\n## Background\n- Oracle transferred Java EE to the Eclipse Foundation\n- Eclipse Foundation created Jakarta EE\n- Due to trademark issues, the `javax` namespace couldn't be used\n- All packages were renamed from `javax.*` to `jakarta.*`\n\n## Common Package Migrations\n\n### 1. JPA / Persistence\n```java\n// Old (javax)\nimport javax.persistence.Entity;\nimport javax.persistence.Id;\nimport javax.persistence.GeneratedValue;\nimport javax.persistence.GenerationType;\nimport javax.persistence.Table;\nimport javax.persistence.Column;\nimport javax.persistence.Enumerated;\nimport javax.persistence.EnumType;\nimport javax.persistence.PrePersist;\nimport javax.persistence.PreUpdate;\nimport javax.persistence.ManyToOne;\nimport javax.persistence.OneToMany;\nimport javax.persistence.JoinColumn;\n\n// New (jakarta)\nimport jakarta.persistence.Entity;\nimport jakarta.persistence.Id;\nimport jakarta.persistence.GeneratedValue;\nimport jakarta.persistence.GenerationType;\nimport jakarta.persistence.Table;\nimport jakarta.persistence.Column;\nimport jakarta.persistence.Enumerated;\nimport jakarta.persistence.EnumType;\nimport jakarta.persistence.PrePersist;\nimport jakarta.persistence.PreUpdate;\nimport jakarta.persistence.ManyToOne;\nimport jakarta.persistence.OneToMany;\nimport jakarta.persistence.JoinColumn;\n```\n\n### 2. Bean Validation\n```java\n// Old (javax)\nimport javax.validation.Valid;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotBlank;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotEmpty;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Size;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Min;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Max;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Email;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Pattern;\n\n// New (jakarta)\nimport jakarta.validation.Valid;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.NotEmpty;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Min;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Max;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;\n```\n\n### 3. Servlet API\n```java\n// Old (javax)\nimport javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;\nimport javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;\nimport javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;\nimport javax.servlet.Filter;\nimport javax.servlet.FilterChain;\nimport javax.servlet.ServletException;\n\n// New (jakarta)\nimport jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;\nimport jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;\nimport jakarta.servlet.http.HttpSession;\nimport jakarta.servlet.Filter;\nimport jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;\nimport jakarta.servlet.ServletException;\n```\n\n### 4. Annotations (Dependency Injection)\n```java\n// Old (javax)\nimport javax.annotation.PostConstruct;\nimport javax.annotation.PreDestroy;\nimport javax.inject.Inject;\n\n// New (jakarta)\nimport jakarta.annotation.PostConstruct;\nimport jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;\nimport jakarta.inject.Inject;\n```\n\n### 5. Transaction Management\n```java\n// Old (javax)\nimport javax.transaction.Transactional;\n\n// New (jakarta)\nimport jakarta.transaction.Transactional;\n\n// Note: Spring's @Transactional is still org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional\n```\n\n## Complete Mapping Table\n\n| Old Package (javax.*) | New Package (jakarta.*) |\n|----------------------|-------------------------|\n| `javax.persistence.*` | `jakarta.persistence.*` |\n| `javax.validation.*` | `jakarta.validation.*` |\n| `javax.servlet.*` | `jakarta.servlet.*` |\n| `javax.annotation.*` | `jakarta.annotation.*` |\n| `javax.transaction.*` | `jakarta.transaction.*` |\n| `javax.xml.bind.*` | `jakarta.xml.bind.*` |\n| `javax.ws.rs.*` | `jakarta.ws.rs.*` |\n| `javax.mail.*` | `jakarta.mail.*` |\n| `javax.jms.*` | `jakarta.jms.*` |\n| `javax.ejb.*` | `jakarta.ejb.*` |\n\n## Automated Migration Tools\n\n### 1. IDE Find & Replace\nMost IDEs support project-wide find and replace:\n\n**IntelliJ IDEA:**\n- Press `Ctrl+Shift+R` (Windows/Linux) or `Cmd+Shift+R` (Mac)\n- Find: `javax.`\n- Replace: `jakarta.`\n- Scope: Whole project\n- File mask: `*.java`\n\n**Eclipse:**\n- Press `Ctrl+H`\n- Choose \"File Search\"\n- Containing text: `javax.`\n- File name patterns: `*.java`\n- Use \"Replace\" button\n\n**VS Code:**\n- Press `Ctrl+Shift+H`\n- Find: `javax.`\n- Replace: `jakarta.`\n- Include: `**/*.java`\n\n### 2. OpenRewrite Recipe\nAdd OpenRewrite plugin to automate migration:\n\n```xml\n<build>\n    <plugins>\n        <plugin>\n            <groupId>org.openrewrite.maven</groupId>\n            <artifactId>rewrite-maven-plugin</artifactId>\n            <version>5.20.0</version>\n            <configuration>\n                <activeRecipes>\n                    <recipe>org.openrewrite.java.spring.boot3.UpgradeSpringBoot_3_2</recipe>\n                </activeRecipes>\n            </configuration>\n            <dependencies>\n                <dependency>\n                    <groupId>org.openrewrite.recipe</groupId>\n                    <artifactId>rewrite-spring</artifactId>\n                    <version>5.7.0</version>\n                </dependency>\n            </dependencies>\n        </plugin>\n    </plugins>\n</build>\n```\n\nRun with: `mvn rewrite:run`\n\n### 3. Manual Search with grep/sed\n```bash\n# Find all files with javax imports\ngrep -r \"import javax\\.\" src/\n\n# Replace using sed (backup files first!)\nfind src/ -name \"*.java\" -exec sed -i 's/import javax\\./import jakarta\\./g' {} \\;\n```\n\n## Example Migration\n\n### Before (Spring Boot 2.7):\n```java\npackage com.example.userservice.model;\n\nimport javax.persistence.*;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Email;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotBlank;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Size;\nimport java.time.LocalDateTime;\n\n@Entity\n@Table(name = \"users\")\npublic class User {\n\n    @Id\n    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)\n    private Long id;\n\n    @NotBlank(message = \"Username is required\")\n    @Size(min = 3, max = 50)\n    @Column(unique = true, nullable = false)\n    private String username;\n\n    @NotBlank(message = \"Email is required\")\n    @Email(message = \"Invalid email format\")\n    @Column(unique = true, nullable = false)\n    private String email;\n\n    // ... rest of the class\n}\n```\n\n### After (Spring Boot 3.2):\n```java\npackage com.example.userservice.model;\n\nimport jakarta.persistence.*;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;\nimport java.time.LocalDateTime;\n\n@Entity\n@Table(name = \"users\")\npublic class User {\n\n    @Id\n    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)\n    private Long id;\n\n    @NotBlank(message = \"Username is required\")\n    @Size(min = 3, max = 50)\n    @Column(unique = true, nullable = false)\n    private String username;\n\n    @NotBlank(message = \"Email is required\")\n    @Email(message = \"Invalid email format\")\n    @Column(unique = true, nullable = false)\n    private String email;\n\n    // ... rest of the class\n}\n```\n\n## Validation After Migration\n\n### 1. Compile Check\n```bash\nmvn clean compile\n```\n\nIf you see errors like:\n```\npackage javax.persistence does not exist\n```\n\nThen you have missed some imports.\n\n### 2. Search for Remaining javax Imports\n```bash\n# Should return no results\ngrep -r \"import javax\\.\" src/main/java/\ngrep -r \"import javax\\.\" src/test/java/\n```\n\n### 3. Check Configuration Files\nSome configuration files might reference javax classes:\n- `persistence.xml`\n- `web.xml`\n- Custom XML configurations\n\n## Common Issues\n\n### Issue 1: Mixed Dependencies\n**Problem**: Some third-party libraries still use `javax.*`\n**Solution**:\n- Update all dependencies to Jakarta EE 9+ compatible versions\n- Check library compatibility before migrating\n- Use dependency management to ensure consistent versions\n\n### Issue 2: Reflection and Class.forName()\n**Problem**: String references to class names still use \"javax\"\n```java\nClass.forName(\"javax.persistence.Entity\"); // Won't work\n```\n**Solution**: Update string references\n```java\nClass.forName(\"jakarta.persistence.Entity\");\n```\n\n### Issue 3: Annotation Processing\n**Problem**: Custom annotation processors might reference javax packages\n**Solution**: Update annotation processors to use jakarta packages\n\n## Migration Checklist\n\n- [ ] Backup project before migration\n- [ ] Update all `javax.persistence.*` to `jakarta.persistence.*`\n- [ ] Update all `javax.validation.*` to `jakarta.validation.*`\n- [ ] Update all `javax.servlet.*` to `jakarta.servlet.*`\n- [ ] Update all `javax.annotation.*` to `jakarta.annotation.*`\n- [ ] Search for any remaining `javax.` imports\n- [ ] Update string references in reflection code\n- [ ] Run `mvn clean compile` to verify\n- [ ] Run full test suite\n- [ ] Check for third-party library compatibility\n\n## References\n- [Jakarta EE 9 Release](https://jakarta.ee/release/9/)\n- [Jakarta EE Specifications](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/)\n- [Spring Boot 3.0 Migration - Jakarta EE](https://github.com/spring-projects/spring-boot/wiki/Spring-Boot-3.0-Migration-Guide#jakarta-ee)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2171,"cache_read_input_tokens":28231,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2171,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6f9f7172-e84f-4a4a-a2a4-a3ac0561d4b4","uuid":"f9251349-b309-46bf-9f04-94429eea0ab6"}

This is one of the most significant changes when migrating to Spring Boot 3.x.\n\n## Background\n- Oracle transferred Java EE to the Eclipse Foundation\n- Eclipse Foundation created Jakarta EE\n- Due to trademark issues, the `javax` namespace couldn't be used\n- All packages were renamed from `javax.*` to `jakarta.*`\n\n## Common Package Migrations\n\n### 1. JPA / Persistence\n```java\n// Old (javax)\nimport javax.persistence.Entity;\nimport javax.persistence.Id;\nimport javax.persistence.GeneratedValue;\nimport javax.persistence.GenerationType;\nimport javax.persistence.Table;\nimport javax.persistence.Column;\nimport javax.persistence.Enumerated;\nimport javax.persistence.EnumType;\nimport javax.persistence.PrePersist;\nimport javax.persistence.PreUpdate;\nimport javax.persistence.ManyToOne;\nimport javax.persistence.OneToMany;\nimport javax.persistence.JoinColumn;\n\n// New (jakarta)\nimport jakarta.persistence.Entity;\nimport jakarta.persistence.Id;\nimport jakarta.persistence.GeneratedValue;\nimport jakarta.persistence.GenerationType;\nimport jakarta.persistence.Table;\nimport jakarta.persistence.Column;\nimport jakarta.persistence.Enumerated;\nimport jakarta.persistence.EnumType;\nimport jakarta.persistence.PrePersist;\nimport jakarta.persistence.PreUpdate;\nimport jakarta.persistence.ManyToOne;\nimport jakarta.persistence.OneToMany;\nimport jakarta.persistence.JoinColumn;\n```\n\n### 2. Bean Validation\n```java\n// Old (javax)\nimport javax.validation.Valid;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotNull;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotBlank;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotEmpty;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Size;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Min;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Max;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Email;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Pattern;\n\n// New (jakarta)\nimport jakarta.validation.Valid;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.NotEmpty;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Min;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Max;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;\n```\n\n### 3. Servlet API\n```java\n// Old (javax)\nimport javax.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;\nimport javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;\nimport javax.servlet.http.HttpSession;\nimport javax.servlet.Filter;\nimport javax.servlet.FilterChain;\nimport javax.servlet.ServletException;\n\n// New (jakarta)\nimport jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;\nimport jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;\nimport jakarta.servlet.http.HttpSession;\nimport jakarta.servlet.Filter;\nimport jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;\nimport jakarta.servlet.ServletException;\n```\n\n### 4. Annotations (Dependency Injection)\n```java\n// Old (javax)\nimport javax.annotation.PostConstruct;\nimport javax.annotation.PreDestroy;\nimport javax.inject.Inject;\n\n// New (jakarta)\nimport jakarta.annotation.PostConstruct;\nimport jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;\nimport jakarta.inject.Inject;\n```\n\n### 5. Transaction Management\n```java\n// Old (javax)\nimport javax.transaction.Transactional;\n\n// New (jakarta)\nimport jakarta.transaction.Transactional;\n\n// Note: Spring's @Transactional is still org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional\n```\n\n## Complete Mapping Table\n\n| Old Package (javax.*) | New Package (jakarta.*) |\n|----------------------|-------------------------|\n| `javax.persistence.*` | `jakarta.persistence.*` |\n| `javax.validation.*` | `jakarta.validation.*` |\n| `javax.servlet.*` | `jakarta.servlet.*` |\n| `javax.annotation.*` | `jakarta.annotation.*` |\n| `javax.transaction.*` | `jakarta.transaction.*` |\n| `javax.xml.bind.*` | `jakarta.xml.bind.*` |\n| `javax.ws.rs.*` | `jakarta.ws.rs.*` |\n| `javax.mail.*` | `jakarta.mail.*` |\n| `javax.jms.*` | `jakarta.jms.*` |\n| `javax.ejb.*` | `jakarta.ejb.*` |\n\n## Automated Migration Tools\n\n### 1. IDE Find & Replace\nMost IDEs support project-wide find and replace:\n\n**IntelliJ IDEA:**\n- Press `Ctrl+Shift+R` (Windows/Linux) or `Cmd+Shift+R` (Mac)\n- Find: `javax.`\n- Replace: `jakarta.`\n- Scope: Whole project\n- File mask: `*.java`\n\n**Eclipse:**\n- Press `Ctrl+H`\n- Choose \"File Search\"\n- Containing text: `javax.`\n- File name patterns: `*.java`\n- Use \"Replace\" button\n\n**VS Code:**\n- Press `Ctrl+Shift+H`\n- Find: `javax.`\n- Replace: `jakarta.`\n- Include: `**/*.java`\n\n### 2. OpenRewrite Recipe\nAdd OpenRewrite plugin to automate migration:\n\n```xml\n<build>\n    <plugins>\n        <plugin>\n            <groupId>org.openrewrite.maven</groupId>\n            <artifactId>rewrite-maven-plugin</artifactId>\n            <version>5.20.0</version>\n            <configuration>\n                <activeRecipes>\n                    <recipe>org.openrewrite.java.spring.boot3.UpgradeSpringBoot_3_2</recipe>\n                </activeRecipes>\n            </configuration>\n            <dependencies>\n                <dependency>\n                    <groupId>org.openrewrite.recipe</groupId>\n                    <artifactId>rewrite-spring</artifactId>\n                    <version>5.7.0</version>\n                </dependency>\n            </dependencies>\n        </plugin>\n    </plugins>\n</build>\n```\n\nRun with: `mvn rewrite:run`\n\n### 3. Manual Search with grep/sed\n```bash\n# Find all files with javax imports\ngrep -r \"import javax\\.\" src/\n\n# Replace using sed (backup files first!)\nfind src/ -name \"*.java\" -exec sed -i 's/import javax\\./import jakarta\\./g' {} \\;\n```\n\n## Example Migration\n\n### Before (Spring Boot 2.7):\n```java\npackage com.example.userservice.model;\n\nimport javax.persistence.*;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Email;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.NotBlank;\nimport javax.validation.constraints.Size;\nimport java.time.LocalDateTime;\n\n@Entity\n@Table(name = \"users\")\npublic class User {\n\n    @Id\n    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)\n    private Long id;\n\n    @NotBlank(message = \"Username is required\")\n    @Size(min = 3, max = 50)\n    @Column(unique = true, nullable = false)\n    private String username;\n\n    @NotBlank(message = \"Email is required\")\n    @Email(message = \"Invalid email format\")\n    @Column(unique = true, nullable = false)\n    private String email;\n\n    // ... rest of the class\n}\n```\n\n### After (Spring Boot 3.2):\n```java\npackage com.example.userservice.model;\n\nimport jakarta.persistence.*;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;\nimport jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;\nimport java.time.LocalDateTime;\n\n@Entity\n@Table(name = \"users\")\npublic class User {\n\n    @Id\n    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)\n    private Long id;\n\n    @NotBlank(message = \"Username is required\")\n    @Size(min = 3, max = 50)\n    @Column(unique = true, nullable = false)\n    private String username;\n\n    @NotBlank(message = \"Email is required\")\n    @Email(message = \"Invalid email format\")\n    @Column(unique = true, nullable = false)\n    private String email;\n\n    // ... rest of the class\n}\n```\n\n## Validation After Migration\n\n### 1. Compile Check\n```bash\nmvn clean compile\n```\n\nIf you see errors like:\n```\npackage javax.persistence does not exist\n```\n\nThen you have missed some imports.\n\n### 2. Search for Remaining javax Imports\n```bash\n# Should return no results\ngrep -r \"import javax\\.\" src/main/java/\ngrep -r \"import javax\\.\" src/test/java/\n```\n\n### 3. Check Configuration Files\nSome configuration files might reference javax classes:\n- `persistence.xml`\n- `web.xml`\n- Custom XML configurations\n\n## Common Issues\n\n### Issue 1: Mixed Dependencies\n**Problem**: Some third-party libraries still use `javax.*`\n**Solution**:\n- Update all dependencies to Jakarta EE 9+ compatible versions\n- Check library compatibility before migrating\n- Use dependency management to ensure consistent versions\n\n### Issue 2: Reflection and Class.forName()\n**Problem**: String references to class names still use \"javax\"\n```java\nClass.forName(\"javax.persistence.Entity\"); // Won't work\n```\n**Solution**: Update string references\n```java\nClass.forName(\"jakarta.persistence.Entity\");\n```\n\n### Issue 3: Annotation Processing\n**Problem**: Custom annotation processors might reference javax packages\n**Solution**: Update annotation processors to use jakarta packages\n\n## Migration Checklist\n\n- [ ] Backup project before migration\n- [ ] Update all `javax.persistence.*` to `jakarta.persistence.*`\n- [ ] Update all `javax.validation.*` to `jakarta.validation.*`\n- [ ] Update all `javax.servlet.*` to `jakarta.servlet.*`\n- [ ] Update all `javax.annotation.*` to `jakarta.annotation.*`\n- [ ] Search for any remaining `javax.` imports\n- [ ] Update string references in reflection code\n- [ ] Run `mvn clean compile` to verify\n- [ ] Run full test suite\n- [ ] Check for third-party library compatibility\n\n## References\n- [Jakarta EE 9 Release](https://jakarta.ee/release/9/)\n- [Jakarta EE Specifications](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/)\n- [Spring Boot 3.0 Migration - Jakarta EE](https://github.com/spring-projects/spring-boot/wiki/Spring-Boot-3.0-Migration-Guide#jakarta-ee)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

The most notable change is the removal of `WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter` and the shift to component-based configuration.\n\n## Major Changes\n\n### 1. WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter Removed\n\nThe `WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter` class has been removed. The old pattern of extending this class is no longer supported.\n\n#### Old Pattern (Spring Security 5.x):\n```java\n@Configuration\n@EnableWebSecurity\npublic class SecurityConfig extends WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter {\n\n    @Override\n    protected void configure(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n        http\n            .authorizeRequests()\n                .antMatchers(\"/public/**\").permitAll()\n                .anyRequest().authenticated();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    protected void configure(AuthenticationManagerBuilder auth) throws Exception {\n        auth.userDetailsService(userDetailsService)\n            .passwordEncoder(passwordEncoder());\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    @Bean\n    public AuthenticationManager authenticationManagerBean() throws Exception {\n        return super.authenticationManagerBean();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### New Pattern (Spring Security 6.x):\n```java\n@Configuration\n@EnableWebSecurity\npublic class SecurityConfig {\n\n    @Bean\n    public SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n        http\n            .authorizeHttpRequests(auth -> auth\n                .requestMatchers(\"/public/**\").permitAll()\n                .anyRequest().authenticated()\n            );\n        return http.build();\n    }\n\n    @Bean\n    public AuthenticationManager authenticationManager(\n            AuthenticationConfiguration authenticationConfiguration) throws Exception {\n        return authenticationConfiguration.getAuthenticationManager();\n    }\n\n    @Bean\n    public PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder() {\n        return new BCryptPasswordEncoder();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Lambda DSL (Recommended Style)\n\nSpring Security 6 promotes the use of lambda DSL for cleaner configuration.\n\n#### Old Style:\n```java\nhttp\n    .csrf().disable()\n    .authorizeRequests()\n        .antMatchers(\"/api/public/**\").permitAll()\n        .anyRequest().authenticated()\n    .and()\n    .sessionManagement()\n        .sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS);\n```\n\n#### New Lambda Style:\n```java\nhttp\n    .csrf(csrf -> csrf.disable())\n    .authorizeHttpRequests(auth -> auth\n        .requestMatchers(\"/api/public/**\").permitAll()\n        .anyRequest().authenticated()\n    )\n    .sessionManagement(session -> session\n        .sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS)\n    );\n```\n\n### 3. Request Matchers Changes\n\n#### antMatchers() → requestMatchers()\n```java\n// Old\n.antMatchers(\"/api/**\").permitAll()\n.antMatchers(HttpMethod.POST, \"/api/users\").permitAll()\n\n// New\n.requestMatchers(\"/api/**\").permitAll()\n.requestMatchers(HttpMethod.POST, \"/api/users\").permitAll()\n```\n\n#### mvcMatchers() → requestMatchers()\n```java\n// Old\n.mvcMatchers(\"/api/**\").permitAll()\n\n// New (uses MvcRequestMatcher)\n.requestMatchers(\"/api/**\").permitAll()\n```\n\n### 4. Method Security Changes\n\n#### Old Annotation:\n```java\n@EnableGlobalMethodSecurity(prePostEnabled = true)\n```\n\n#### New Annotation:\n```java\n@EnableMethodSecurity\n```\n\nThe `@EnableMethodSecurity` annotation:\n- Enables `@PreAuthorize`, `@PostAuthorize`, `@PreFilter`, `@PostFilter` by default\n- Provides better performance\n- More flexible configuration\n\n### 5. Complete Migration Example\n\n#### Before (Spring Security 5.x):\n```java\n@Configuration\n@EnableWebSecurity\n@EnableGlobalMethodSecurity(prePostEnabled = true)\npublic class SecurityConfig extends WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter {\n\n    @Autowired\n    private UserDetailsService userDetailsService;\n\n    @Bean\n    public PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder() {\n        return new BCryptPasswordEncoder();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    protected void configure(AuthenticationManagerBuilder auth) throws Exception {\n        auth.userDetailsService(userDetailsService)\n            .passwordEncoder(passwordEncoder());\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    @Bean\n    public AuthenticationManager authenticationManagerBean() throws Exception {\n        return super.authenticationManagerBean();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    protected void configure(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n        http\n            .csrf().disable()\n            .sessionManagement()\n                .sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS)\n            .and()\n            .exceptionHandling()\n                .authenticationEntryPoint((request, response, ex) -> {\n                    response.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED, ex.getMessage());\n                })\n            .and()\n            .authorizeRequests()\n                .antMatchers(HttpMethod.POST, \"/api/users\").permitAll()\n                .antMatchers(\"/api/auth/**\").permitAll()\n                .antMatchers(\"/h2-console/**\").permitAll()\n                .anyRequest().authenticated()\n            .and()\n            .headers().frameOptions().disable();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### After (Spring Security 6.x):\n```java\n@Configuration\n@EnableWebSecurity\n@EnableMethodSecurity\npublic class SecurityConfig {\n\n    @Bean\n    public SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n        http\n            .csrf(csrf -> csrf.disable())\n            .sessionManagement(session -> session\n                .sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS)\n            )\n            .exceptionHandling(exception -> exception\n                .authenticationEntryPoint((request, response, ex) -> {\n                    response.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED, ex.getMessage());\n                })\n            )\n            .authorizeHttpRequests(auth -> auth\n                .requestMatchers(HttpMethod.POST, \"/api/users\").permitAll()\n                .requestMatchers(\"/api/auth/**\").permitAll()\n                .requestMatchers(\"/h2-console/**\").permitAll()\n                .anyRequest().authenticated()\n            )\n            .headers(headers -> headers\n                .frameOptions(frame -> frame.disable())\n            );\n\n        return http.build();\n    }\n\n    @Bean\n    public AuthenticationManager authenticationManager(\n            AuthenticationConfiguration authenticationConfiguration) throws Exception {\n        return authenticationConfiguration.getAuthenticationManager();\n    }\n\n    @Bean\n    public PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder() {\n        return new BCryptPasswordEncoder();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 6. UserDetailsService Configuration\n\n#### Old Way (via configure method):\n```java\n@Override\nprotected void configure(AuthenticationManagerBuilder auth) throws Exception {\n    auth.userDetailsService(userDetailsService)\n        .passwordEncoder(passwordEncoder());\n}\n```\n\n#### New Way (via bean):\nThe `UserDetailsService` is automatically picked up when defined as a bean:\n```java\n@Service\npublic class CustomUserDetailsService implements UserDetailsService {\n    @Override\n    public UserDetails loadUserByUsername(String username) throws UsernameNotFoundException {\n        // Implementation\n    }\n}\n```\n\nOr configure explicitly:\n```java\n@Bean\npublic AuthenticationProvider authenticationProvider(\n        UserDetailsService userDetailsService,\n        PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder) {\n    DaoAuthenticationProvider provider = new DaoAuthenticationProvider();\n    provider.setUserDetailsService(userDetailsService);\n    provider.setPasswordEncoder(passwordEncoder);\n    return provider;\n}\n```\n\n### 7. Common Configurations\n\n#### CORS Configuration:\n```java\n@Bean\npublic SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n    http\n        .cors(cors -> cors.configurationSource(corsConfigurationSource()))\n        // ... other config\n    return http.build();\n}\n\n@Bean\npublic CorsConfigurationSource corsConfigurationSource() {\n    CorsConfiguration configuration = new CorsConfiguration();\n    configuration.setAllowedOrigins(List.of(\"http://localhost:3000\"));\n    configuration.setAllowedMethods(List.of(\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"DELETE\"));\n    configuration.setAllowedHeaders(List.of(\"*\"));\n    UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource source = new UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource();\n    source.registerCorsConfiguration(\"/**\", configuration);\n    return source;\n}\n```\n\n#### JWT Filter:\n```java\n@Bean\npublic SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(\n        HttpSecurity http,\n        JwtAuthenticationFilter jwtFilter) throws Exception {\n    http\n        .csrf(csrf -> csrf.disable())\n        .sessionManagement(session -> session\n            .sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS)\n        )\n        .authorizeHttpRequests(auth -> auth\n            .requestMatchers(\"/api/auth/**\").permitAll()\n            .anyRequest().authenticated()\n        )\n        .addFilterBefore(jwtFilter, UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter.class);\n\n    return http.build();\n}\n```\n\n### 8. HttpServletResponse Import Change\n\nDue to Jakarta EE migration:\n```java\n// Old\nimport javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;\n\n// New\nimport jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;\n```\n\n### 9. Deprecated Methods\n\nSeveral methods have been deprecated or removed:\n\n| Old Method | New Method |\n|-----------|------------|\n| `authorizeRequests()` | `authorizeHttpRequests()` |\n| `antMatchers()` | `requestMatchers()` |\n| `mvcMatchers()` | `requestMatchers()` |\n| `regexMatchers()` | `requestMatchers()` (with regex pattern) |\n| `and()` | Not needed with lambda DSL |\n\n### 10. Testing Changes\n\n#### Old Way:\n```java\n@WithMockUser(username = \"admin\", roles = {\"ADMIN\"})\n```\n\n#### New Way (same annotation, but configuration changes):\n```java\n@WithMockUser(username = \"admin\", roles = {\"ADMIN\"})\n```\n\nThe annotation remains the same, but ensure proper imports:\n```java\nimport org.springframework.security.test.context.support.WithMockUser;\n```\n\n## Migration Checklist\n\n- [ ] Remove `extends WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter`\n- [ ] Create `SecurityFilterChain` bean\n- [ ] Replace `configure(HttpSecurity)` with `securityFilterChain()` bean\n- [ ] Replace `configure(AuthenticationManagerBuilder)` with `AuthenticationManager` bean\n- [ ] Change `@EnableGlobalMethodSecurity` to `@EnableMethodSecurity`\n- [ ] Replace `antMatchers()` with `requestMatchers()`\n- [ ] Replace `authorizeRequests()` with `authorizeHttpRequests()`\n- [ ] Convert to lambda DSL (recommended)\n- [ ] Update `javax.servlet.*` imports to `jakarta.servlet.*`\n- [ ] Test all security configurations\n- [ ] Verify authentication and authorization work correctly\n\n## Common Issues\n\n### Issue 1: AuthenticationManager Bean Not Found\n**Solution**: Create AuthenticationManager bean explicitly:\n```java\n@Bean\npublic AuthenticationManager authenticationManager(\n        AuthenticationConfiguration config) throws Exception {\n    return config.getAuthenticationManager();\n}\n```\n\n### Issue 2: Circular Dependencies\n**Solution**: Use lazy injection or refactor configuration:\n```java\n@Bean\npublic SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(\n        HttpSecurity http,\n        @Lazy JwtAuthenticationFilter jwtFilter) throws Exception {\n    // configuration\n}\n```\n\n### Issue 3: Multiple SecurityFilterChain Beans\n**Solution**: Use `@Order` annotation to specify order:\n```java\n@Bean\n@Order(1)\npublic SecurityFilterChain apiSecurityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n    http.securityMatcher(\"/api/**\")\n        // configuration for /api/**\n    return http.build();\n}\n\n@Bean\n@Order(2)\npublic SecurityFilterChain defaultSecurityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n    // default configuration\n    return http.build();\n}\n```\n\n## References\n- [Spring Security 6.0 Migration Guide](https://docs.spring.io/spring-security/reference/6.0/migration/index.html)\n- [Spring Security without WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter](https://spring.io/blog/2022/02/21/spring-security-without-the-websecurityconfigureradapter)\n- [Lambda DSL super.authenticationManagerBean();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    protected void configure(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n        http\n            .csrf().disable()\n            .sessionManagement()\n                .sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS)\n            .and()\n            .exceptionHandling()\n                .authenticationEntryPoint((request, response, ex) -> {\n                    response.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED, ex.getMessage());\n                })\n            .and()\n            .authorizeRequests()\n                .antMatchers(HttpMethod.POST, \"/api/users\").permitAll()\n                .antMatchers(\"/api/auth/**\").permitAll()\n                .antMatchers(\"/h2-console/**\").permitAll()\n                .anyRequest().authenticated()\n            .and()\n            .headers().frameOptions().disable();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### After (Spring Security 6.x):\n```java\n@Configuration\n@EnableWebSecurity\n@EnableMethodSecurity\npublic class SecurityConfig {\n\n    @Bean\n    public SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n        http\n            .csrf(csrf -> csrf.disable())\n            .sessionManagement(session -> session\n                .sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS)\n            )\n            .exceptionHandling(exception -> exception\n                .authenticationEntryPoint((request, response, ex) -> {\n                    response.sendError(HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED, ex.getMessage());\n                })\n            )\n            .authorizeHttpRequests(auth -> auth\n                .requestMatchers(HttpMethod.POST, \"/api/users\").permitAll()\n                .requestMatchers(\"/api/auth/**\").permitAll()\n                .requestMatchers(\"/h2-console/**\").permitAll()\n                .anyRequest().authenticated()\n            )\n            .headers(headers -> headers\n                .frameOptions(frame -> frame.disable())\n            );\n\n        return http.build();\n    }\n\n    @Bean\n    public AuthenticationManager authenticationManager(\n            AuthenticationConfiguration authenticationConfiguration) throws Exception {\n        return authenticationConfiguration.getAuthenticationManager();\n    }\n\n    @Bean\n    public PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder() {\n        return new BCryptPasswordEncoder();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 6. UserDetailsService Configuration\n\n#### Old Way (via configure method):\n```java\n@Override\nprotected void configure(AuthenticationManagerBuilder auth) throws Exception {\n    auth.userDetailsService(userDetailsService)\n        .passwordEncoder(passwordEncoder());\n}\n```\n\n#### New Way (via bean):\nThe `UserDetailsService` is automatically picked up when defined as a bean:\n```java\n@Service\npublic class CustomUserDetailsService implements UserDetailsService {\n    @Override\n    public UserDetails loadUserByUsername(String username) throws UsernameNotFoundException {\n        // Implementation\n    }\n}\n```\n\nOr configure explicitly:\n```java\n@Bean\npublic AuthenticationProvider authenticationProvider(\n        UserDetailsService userDetailsService,\n        PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder) {\n    DaoAuthenticationProvider provider = new DaoAuthenticationProvider();\n    provider.setUserDetailsService(userDetailsService);\n    provider.setPasswordEncoder(passwordEncoder);\n    return provider;\n}\n```\n\n### 7. Common Configurations\n\n#### CORS Configuration:\n```java\n@Bean\npublic SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n    http\n        .cors(cors -> cors.configurationSource(corsConfigurationSource()))\n        // ... other config\n    return http.build();\n}\n\n@Bean\npublic CorsConfigurationSource corsConfigurationSource() {\n    CorsConfiguration configuration = new CorsConfiguration();\n    configuration.setAllowedOrigins(List.of(\"http://localhost:3000\"));\n    configuration.setAllowedMethods(List.of(\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"DELETE\"));\n    configuration.setAllowedHeaders(List.of(\"*\"));\n    UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource source = new UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource();\n    source.registerCorsConfiguration(\"/**\", configuration);\n    return source;\n}\n```\n\n#### JWT Filter:\n```java\n@Bean\npublic SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(\n        HttpSecurity http,\n        JwtAuthenticationFilter jwtFilter) throws Exception {\n    http\n        .csrf(csrf -> csrf.disable())\n        .sessionManagement(session -> session\n            .sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS)\n        )\n        .authorizeHttpRequests(auth -> auth\n            .requestMatchers(\"/api/auth/**\").permitAll()\n            .anyRequest().authenticated()\n        )\n        .addFilterBefore(jwtFilter, UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter.class);\n\n    return http.build();\n}\n```\n\n### 8. HttpServletResponse Import Change\n\nDue to Jakarta EE migration:\n```java\n// Old\nimport javax.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;\n\n// New\nimport jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;\n```\n\n### 9. Deprecated Methods\n\nSeveral methods have been deprecated or removed:\n\n| Old Method | New Method |\n|-----------|------------|\n| `authorizeRequests()` | `authorizeHttpRequests()` |\n| `antMatchers()` | `requestMatchers()` |\n| `mvcMatchers()` | `requestMatchers()` |\n| `regexMatchers()` | `requestMatchers()` (with regex pattern) |\n| `and()` | Not needed with lambda DSL |\n\n### 10. Testing Changes\n\n#### Old Way:\n```java\n@WithMockUser(username = \"admin\", roles = {\"ADMIN\"})\n```\n\n#### New Way (same annotation, but configuration changes):\n```java\n@WithMockUser(username = \"admin\", roles = {\"ADMIN\"})\n```\n\nThe annotation remains the same, but ensure proper imports:\n```java\nimport org.springframework.security.test.context.support.WithMockUser;\n```\n\n## Migration Checklist\n\n- [ ] Remove `extends WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter`\n- [ ] Create `SecurityFilterChain` bean\n- [ ] Replace `configure(HttpSecurity)` with `securityFilterChain()` bean\n- [ ] Replace `configure(AuthenticationManagerBuilder)` with `AuthenticationManager` bean\n- [ ] Change `@EnableGlobalMethodSecurity` to `@EnableMethodSecurity`\n- [ ] Replace `antMatchers()` with `requestMatchers()`\n- [ ] Replace `authorizeRequests()` with `authorizeHttpRequests()`\n- [ ] Convert to lambda DSL (recommended)\n- [ ] Update `javax.servlet.*` imports to `jakarta.servlet.*`\n- [ ] Test all security configurations\n- [ ] Verify authentication and authorization work correctly\n\n## Common Issues\n\n### Issue 1: AuthenticationManager Bean Not Found\n**Solution**: Create AuthenticationManager bean explicitly:\n```java\n@Bean\npublic AuthenticationManager authenticationManager(\n        AuthenticationConfiguration config) throws Exception {\n    return config.getAuthenticationManager();\n}\n```\n\n### Issue 2: Circular Dependencies\n**Solution**: Use lazy injection or refactor configuration:\n```java\n@Bean\npublic SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(\n        HttpSecurity http,\n        @Lazy JwtAuthenticationFilter jwtFilter) throws Exception {\n    // configuration\n}\n```\n\n### Issue 3: Multiple SecurityFilterChain Beans\n**Solution**: Use `@Order` annotation to specify order:\n```java\n@Bean\n@Order(1)\npublic SecurityFilterChain apiSecurityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n    http.securityMatcher(\"/api/**\")\n        // configuration for /api/**\n    return http.build();\n}\n\n@Bean\n@Order(2)\npublic SecurityFilterChain defaultSecurityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {\n    // default configuration\n    return http.build();\n}\n```\n\n## References\n- [Spring Security 6.0 Migration Guide](https://docs.spring.io/spring-security/reference/6.0/migration/index.html)\n- [Spring Security without WebSecurityConfigurerAdapter](https://spring.io/blog/2022/02/21/spring-security-without-the-websecurityconfigureradapter)\n- [Lambda DSL Documentation](https://docs.spring.io/spring-security/reference/servlet/configuration/java.html)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01EWgAMWv1UvdeBRmWNNywdM","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01G6XrE5a7AFgs9URC1NxstP","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/workspace/environment/skills/resttemplate-to-restclient-migration.md","content":"# RestTemplate to RestClient Migration Guide\n\n## Overview\nSpring Framework 6.1 (included in Spring Boot 3.2+) introduces `RestClient` as a modern, fluent alternative to `RestTemplate`. While `RestTemplate` is not deprecated, `RestClient` provides a better developer experience with a more intuitive API.\n\n## Why Migrate?\n\n### RestClient Advantages:\n1. **Fluent API**: More readable and chainable method calls\n2. **Better error handling**: More consistent exception handling\n3. **Modern design**: Built with latest Spring features\n4. **Type safety**: Better type inference and compile-time checks\n5. **Simplified configuration**: Easier to configure and customize\n\n### RestTemplate Issues:\n- Verbose API requiring multiple method calls\n- Inconsistent error handling\n- Less intuitive for complex requests\n- Older design patterns\n\n## Basic Comparison\n\n### GET Request\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\nRestTemplate restTemplate = new RestTemplate();\n\n// Simple GET\nString url = \"https://api.example.com/users/1\";\nResponseEntity<User> response = restTemplate.getForEntity(url, User.class);\nUser user = response.getBody();\n\n// GET with headers\nHttpHeaders headers = new HttpHeaders();\nheaders.set(\"Authorization\", \"Bearer token\");\nHttpEntity<Void> entity = new HttpEntity<>(headers);\nResponseEntity<User> response = restTemplate.exchange(url, HttpMethod.GET, entity, User.class);\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nRestClient restClient = RestClient.create();\n\n// Simple GET\nUser user = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(User.class);\n\n// GET with headers\nUser user = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users/1\")\n    .header(\"Authorization\", \"Bearer token\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(User.class);\n```\n\n### POST Request\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\n// POST with body\nString url = \"https://api.example.com/users\";\nUser newUser = new User(\"john\", \"john@example.com\");\n\nHttpHeaders headers = new HttpHeaders();\nheaders.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);\nheaders.setAccept(Collections.singletonList(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON));\n\nHttpEntity<User> request = new HttpEntity<>(newUser, headers);\nResponseEntity<User> response = restTemplate.postForEntity(url, request, User.class);\nUser createdUser = response.getBody();\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nUser newUser = new User(\"john\", \"john@example.com\");\n\nUser createdUser = restClient.post()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users\")\n    .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)\n    .accept(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)\n    .body(newUser)\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(User.class);\n```\n\n### PUT Request\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\nString url = \"https://api.example.com/users/1\";\nUser updatedUser = new User(\"john\", \"newemail@example.com\");\n\nHttpHeaders headers = new HttpHeaders();\nheaders.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);\n\nHttpEntity<User> request = new HttpEntity<>(updatedUser, headers);\nrestTemplate.put(url, request);\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nrestClient.put()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users/1\")\n    .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)\n    .body(updatedUser)\n    .retrieve()\n    .toBodilessEntity();\n```\n\n### DELETE Request\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\nString url = \"https://api.example.com/users/1\";\nrestTemplate.delete(url);\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nrestClient.delete()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .toBodilessEntity();\n```\n\n## Advanced Usage\n\n### 1. Creating RestClient Instance\n\n#### Basic Creation:\n```java\nRestClient restClient = RestClient.create();\n```\n\n#### With Base URL:\n```java\nRestClient restClient = RestClient.create(\"https://api.example.com\");\n```\n\n#### From RestTemplate:\n```java\nRestTemplate restTemplate = new RestTemplate();\nRestClient restClient = RestClient.create(restTemplate);\n```\n\n#### Using Builder:\n```java\nRestClient restClient = RestClient.builder()\n    .baseUrl(\"https://api.example.com\")\n    .defaultHeader(\"User-Agent\", \"MyApp/1.0\")\n    .defaultHeader(\"Accept\", \"application/json\")\n    .build();\n```\n\n### 2. URI Variables\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\nString url = \"https://api.example.com/users/{id}\";\nMap<String, String> uriVariables = new HashMap<>();\nuriVariables.put(\"id\", \"123\");\nResponseEntity<User> response = restTemplate.getForEntity(url, User.class, uriVariables);\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nUser user = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users/{id}\", \"123\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(User.class);\n\n// Or with multiple variables\nUser user = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users/{id}/posts/{postId}\",\n         uriVars -> uriVars.put(\"id\", \"123\").put(\"postId\", \"456\"))\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(User.class);\n```\n\n### 3. Query Parameters\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\nString url = \"https://api.example.com/users?page=1&size=10\";\nResponseEntity<List> response = restTemplate.getForEntity(url, List.class);\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nList<User> users = restClient.get()\n    .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder\n        .path(\"/users\")\n        .queryParam(\"page\", 1)\n        .queryParam(\"size\", 10)\n        .build())\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<List<User>>() {});\n```\n\n### 4. Response Handling\n\n#### Get Response Entity:\n```java\nResponseEntity<User> response = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .toEntity(User.class);\n\nHttpStatusCode status = response.getStatusCode();\nHttpHeaders headers = response.getHeaders();\nUser user = response.getBody();\n```\n\n#### Get Only Body:\n```java\nUser user = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(User.class);\n```\n\n#### Get Bodiless Entity (for DELETE, PUT without response):\n```java\nResponseEntity<Void> response = restClient.delete()\n    .uri(\"/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .toBodilessEntity();\n```\n\n### 5. Error Handling\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\ntry {\n    ResponseEntity<User> response = restTemplate.getForEntity(url, User.class);\n} catch (HttpClientErrorException e) {\n    // Handle 4xx errors\n} catch (HttpServerErrorException e) {\n    // Handle 5xx errors\n} catch (ResourceAccessException e) {\n    // Handle connection errors\n}\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nUser user = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .onStatus(HttpStatusCode::is4xxClientError, (request, response) -> {\n        // Custom 4xx error handling\n        throw new CustomClientException(response.getStatusCode());\n    })\n    .onStatus(HttpStatusCode::is5xxServerError, (request, response) -> {\n        // Custom 5xx error handling\n        throw new CustomServerException(response.getStatusCode());\n    })\n    .body(User.class);\n```\n\n### 6. Generic Types (Lists, Maps)\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\nResponseEntity<List> response = restTemplate.exchange(\n    url,\n    HttpMethod.GET,\n    null,\n    new ParameterizedTypeReference<List<User>>() {}\n);\nList<User> users = response.getBody();\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nList<User> users = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"/users\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<List<User>>() {});\n\n// For Maps\nMap<String, Object> data = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"/data\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<Map<String, Object>>() {});\n```\n\n## Complete Migration Example\n\n### Before (RestTemplate):\n```java\n@Service\npublic class ExternalApiService {\n\n    private final RestTemplate restTemplate;\n\n    @Value(\"${external.api.base-url:https://api.example.com}\")\n    private String baseUrl;\n\n    public ExternalApiService() {\n        this.restTemplate = new RestTemplate();\n    }\n\n    public boolean verifyEmail(String email) {\n        try {\n            String url = baseUrl + \"/verify/email?email=\" + email;\n            ResponseEntity<Map> response = restTemplate.getForEntity(url, Map.class);\n            if (response.getBody() != null) {\n                Object valid = response.getBody().get(\"valid\");\n                return Boolean.TRUE.equals(valid);\n            }\n            return false;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public void sendNotification(String userId, String message) {\n        try {\n            String url = baseUrl + \"/notifications\";\n\n            HttpHeaders headers = new HttpHeaders();\n            headers.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);\n            headers.setAccept(Collections.singletonList(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON));\n\n            Map<String, String> payload = Map.of(\n                \"userId\", userId,\n                \"message\", message,\n                \"type\", \"USER_UPDATE\"\n            );\n\n            HttpEntity<Map<String, String>> request = new HttpEntity<>(payload, headers);\n            restTemplate.postForEntity(url, request, Void.class);\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            System.err.println(\"Failed to send notification: \" + e.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public Map<String, Object> enrichUserProfile(String userId) {\n        try {\n            String url = baseUrl + \"/users/\" + userId + \"/profile\";\n\n            HttpHeaders headers = new HttpHeaders();\n            headers.setAccept(Collections.singletonList(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON));\n\n            HttpEntity<?> request = new HttpEntity<>(headers);\n\n            ResponseEntity<Map> response = restTemplate.exchange(\n                url,\n                HttpMethod.GET,\n                request,\n                Map.class\n            );\n\n            return response.getBody() != null ? response.getBody() : Collections.emptyMap();\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return Collections.emptyMap();\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean requestDataDeletion(String userId) {\n        try {\n            String url = baseUrl + \"/users/\" + userId + \"/data\";\n            restTemplate.delete(url);\n            return true;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### After (RestClient):\n```java\n@Service\npublic class ExternalApiService {\n\n    private final RestClient restClient;\n\n    public ExternalApiService(@Value(\"${external.api.base-url:https://api.example.com}\") String baseUrl) {\n        this.restClient = RestClient.builder()\n            .baseUrl(baseUrl)\n            .defaultHeader(\"Accept\", MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE)\n            .build();\n    }\n\n    public boolean verifyEmail(String email) {\n        try {\n            Map<String, Object> response = restClient.get()\n                .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder\n                    .path(\"/verify/email\")\n                    .queryParam(\"email\", email)\n                    .build())\n                .retrieve()\n                .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<Map<String, Object>>() {});\n\n            if (response != null) {\n                Object valid = response.get(\"valid\");\n                return Boolean.TRUE.equals(valid);\n            }\n            return false;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public void sendNotification(String userId, String message) {\n        try {\n            Map<String, String> payload = Map.of(\n                \"userId\", userId,\n                \"message\", message,\n                \"type\", \"USER_UPDATE\"\n            );\n\n            restClient.post()\n                .uri(\"/notifications\")\n                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)\n                .body(payload)\n                .retrieve()\n                .toBodilessEntity();\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            System.err.println(\"Failed to send notification: \" + e.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public Map<String, Object> enrichUserProfile(String userId) {\n        try {\n            Map<String, Object> response = restClient.get()\n                .uri(\"/users/{userId}/profile\", userId)\n                .retrieve()\n                .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<Map<String, Object>>() {});\n\n            return response != null ? response : Collections.emptyMap();\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return Collections.emptyMap();\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean requestDataDeletion(String userId) {\n        try {\n            restClient.delete()\n                .uri(\"/users/{userId}/data\", userId)\n                .retrieve()\n                .toBodilessEntity();\n            return true;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Configuration as Bean\n\n### Recommended Approach (Spring Boot 3.2+):\n```java\n@Configuration\npublic class RestClientConfig {\n\n    @Bean\n    public RestClient externalApiRestClient(\n            @Value(\"${external.api.base-url}\") String baseUrl) {\n        return RestClient.builder()\n            .baseUrl(baseUrl)\n            .defaultHeader(\"User-Agent\", \"UserService/1.0\")\n            .defaultHeader(\"Accept\", MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE)\n            .build();\n    }\n}\n\n@Service\npublic class ExternalApiService {\n\n    private final RestClient restClient;\n\n    public ExternalApiService(RestClient externalApiRestClient) {\n        this.restClient = externalApiRestClient;\n    }\n\n    // Use restClient\n}\n```\n\n## Migration Checklist\n\n- [ ] Replace `RestTemplate` field with `RestClient`\n- [ ] Update constructor/configuration to create `RestClient` instance\n- [ ] Replace `.getForEntity()` with `.get().retrieve().body()`\n- [ ] Replace `.postForEntity()` with `.post().body().retrieve().body()`\n- [ ] Replace `.put()` with `.put().body().retrieve()`\n- [ ] Replace `.delete()` with `.delete().retrieve()`\n- [ ] Replace `.exchange()` with fluent API equivalents\n- [ ] Update URI building to use `uriBuilder` or path variables\n- [ ] Update error handling to use `.onStatus()`\n- [ ] Update generic types to use `ParameterizedTypeReference`\n- [ ] Test all API calls thoroughly\n\n## References\n- [Spring RestClient
