HttpHeaders();\nheaders.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);\n\nHttpEntity<User> request = new HttpEntity<>(updatedUser, headers);\nrestTemplate.put(url, request);\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nrestClient.put()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users/1\")\n    .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)\n    .body(updatedUser)\n    .retrieve()\n    .toBodilessEntity();\n```\n\n### DELETE Request\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\nString url = \"https://api.example.com/users/1\";\nrestTemplate.delete(url);\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nrestClient.delete()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .toBodilessEntity();\n```\n\n## Advanced Usage\n\n### 1. Creating RestClient Instance\n\n#### Basic Creation:\n```java\nRestClient restClient = RestClient.create();\n```\n\n#### With Base URL:\n```java\nRestClient restClient = RestClient.create(\"https://api.example.com\");\n```\n\n#### From RestTemplate:\n```java\nRestTemplate restTemplate = new RestTemplate();\nRestClient restClient = RestClient.create(restTemplate);\n```\n\n#### Using Builder:\n```java\nRestClient restClient = RestClient.builder()\n    .baseUrl(\"https://api.example.com\")\n    .defaultHeader(\"User-Agent\", \"MyApp/1.0\")\n    .defaultHeader(\"Accept\", \"application/json\")\n    .build();\n```\n\n### 2. URI Variables\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\nString url = \"https://api.example.com/users/{id}\";\nMap<String, String> uriVariables = new HashMap<>();\nuriVariables.put(\"id\", \"123\");\nResponseEntity<User> response = restTemplate.getForEntity(url, User.class, uriVariables);\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nUser user = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users/{id}\", \"123\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(User.class);\n\n// Or with multiple variables\nUser user = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"https://api.example.com/users/{id}/posts/{postId}\",\n         uriVars -> uriVars.put(\"id\", \"123\").put(\"postId\", \"456\"))\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(User.class);\n```\n\n### 3. Query Parameters\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\nString url = \"https://api.example.com/users?page=1&size=10\";\nResponseEntity<List> response = restTemplate.getForEntity(url, List.class);\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nList<User> users = restClient.get()\n    .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder\n        .path(\"/users\")\n        .queryParam(\"page\", 1)\n        .queryParam(\"size\", 10)\n        .build())\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<List<User>>() {});\n```\n\n### 4. Response Handling\n\n#### Get Response Entity:\n```java\nResponseEntity<User> response = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .toEntity(User.class);\n\nHttpStatusCode status = response.getStatusCode();\nHttpHeaders headers = response.getHeaders();\nUser user = response.getBody();\n```\n\n#### Get Only Body:\n```java\nUser user = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(User.class);\n```\n\n#### Get Bodiless Entity (for DELETE, PUT without response):\n```java\nResponseEntity<Void> response = restClient.delete()\n    .uri(\"/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .toBodilessEntity();\n```\n\n### 5. Error Handling\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\ntry {\n    ResponseEntity<User> response = restTemplate.getForEntity(url, User.class);\n} catch (HttpClientErrorException e) {\n    // Handle 4xx errors\n} catch (HttpServerErrorException e) {\n    // Handle 5xx errors\n} catch (ResourceAccessException e) {\n    // Handle connection errors\n}\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nUser user = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"/users/1\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .onStatus(HttpStatusCode::is4xxClientError, (request, response) -> {\n        // Custom 4xx error handling\n        throw new CustomClientException(response.getStatusCode());\n    })\n    .onStatus(HttpStatusCode::is5xxServerError, (request, response) -> {\n        // Custom 5xx error handling\n        throw new CustomServerException(response.getStatusCode());\n    })\n    .body(User.class);\n```\n\n### 6. Generic Types (Lists, Maps)\n\n#### RestTemplate:\n```java\nResponseEntity<List> response = restTemplate.exchange(\n    url,\n    HttpMethod.GET,\n    null,\n    new ParameterizedTypeReference<List<User>>() {}\n);\nList<User> users = response.getBody();\n```\n\n#### RestClient:\n```java\nList<User> users = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"/users\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<List<User>>() {});\n\n// For Maps\nMap<String, Object> data = restClient.get()\n    .uri(\"/data\")\n    .retrieve()\n    .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<Map<String, Object>>() {});\n```\n\n## Complete Migration Example\n\n### Before (RestTemplate):\n```java\n@Service\npublic class ExternalApiService {\n\n    private final RestTemplate restTemplate;\n\n    @Value(\"${external.api.base-url:https://api.example.com}\")\n    private String baseUrl;\n\n    public ExternalApiService() {\n        this.restTemplate = new RestTemplate();\n    }\n\n    public boolean verifyEmail(String email) {\n        try {\n            String url = baseUrl + \"/verify/email?email=\" + email;\n            ResponseEntity<Map> response = restTemplate.getForEntity(url, Map.class);\n            if (response.getBody() != null) {\n                Object valid = response.getBody().get(\"valid\");\n                return Boolean.TRUE.equals(valid);\n            }\n            return false;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public void sendNotification(String userId, String message) {\n        try {\n            String url = baseUrl + \"/notifications\";\n\n            HttpHeaders headers = new HttpHeaders();\n            headers.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);\n            headers.setAccept(Collections.singletonList(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON));\n\n            Map<String, String> payload = Map.of(\n                \"userId\", userId,\n                \"message\", message,\n                \"type\", \"USER_UPDATE\"\n            );\n\n            HttpEntity<Map<String, String>> request = new HttpEntity<>(payload, headers);\n            restTemplate.postForEntity(url, request, Void.class);\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            System.err.println(\"Failed to send notification: \" + e.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public Map<String, Object> enrichUserProfile(String userId) {\n        try {\n            String url = baseUrl + \"/users/\" + userId + \"/profile\";\n\n            HttpHeaders headers = new HttpHeaders();\n            headers.setAccept(Collections.singletonList(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON));\n\n            HttpEntity<?> request = new HttpEntity<>(headers);\n\n            ResponseEntity<Map> response = restTemplate.exchange(\n                url,\n                HttpMethod.GET,\n                request,\n                Map.class\n            );\n\n            return response.getBody() != null ? response.getBody() : Collections.emptyMap();\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return Collections.emptyMap();\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean requestDataDeletion(String userId) {\n        try {\n            String url = baseUrl + \"/users/\" + userId + \"/data\";\n            restTemplate.delete(url);\n            return true;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### After (RestClient):\n```java\n@Service\npublic class ExternalApiService {\n\n    private final RestClient restClient;\n\n    public ExternalApiService(@Value(\"${external.api.base-url:https://api.example.com}\") String baseUrl) {\n        this.restClient = RestClient.builder()\n            .baseUrl(baseUrl)\n            .defaultHeader(\"Accept\", MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE)\n            .build();\n    }\n\n    public boolean verifyEmail(String email) {\n        try {\n            Map<String, Object> response = restClient.get()\n                .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder\n                    .path(\"/verify/email\")\n                    .queryParam(\"email\", email)\n                    .build())\n                .retrieve()\n                .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<Map<String, Object>>() {});\n\n            if (response != null) {\n                Object valid = response.get(\"valid\");\n                return Boolean.TRUE.equals(valid);\n            }\n            return false;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    public void sendNotification(String userId, String message) {\n        try {\n            Map<String, String> payload = Map.of(\n                \"userId\", userId,\n                \"message\", message,\n                \"type\", \"USER_UPDATE\"\n            );\n\n            restClient.post()\n                .uri(\"/notifications\")\n                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)\n                .body(payload)\n                .retrieve()\n                .toBodilessEntity();\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            System.err.println(\"Failed to send notification: \" + e.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public Map<String, Object> enrichUserProfile(String userId) {\n        try {\n            Map<String, Object> response = restClient.get()\n                .uri(\"/users/{userId}/profile\", userId)\n                .retrieve()\n                .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<Map<String, Object>>() {});\n\n            return response != null ? response : Collections.emptyMap();\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return Collections.emptyMap();\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean requestDataDeletion(String userId) {\n        try {\n            restClient.delete()\n                .uri(\"/users/{userId}/data\", userId)\n                .retrieve()\n                .toBodilessEntity();\n            return true;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Configuration as Bean\n\n### Recommended Approach (Spring Boot 3.2+):\n```java\n@Configuration\npublic class RestClientConfig {\n\n    @Bean\n    public RestClient externalApiRestClient(\n            @Value(\"${external.api.base-url}\") String baseUrl) {\n        return RestClient.builder()\n            .baseUrl(baseUrl)\n            .defaultHeader(\"User-Agent\", \"UserService/1.0\")\n            .defaultHeader(\"Accept\", MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE)\n            .build();\n    }\n}\n\n@Service\npublic class ExternalApiService {\n\n    private final RestClient restClient;\n\n    public ExternalApiService(RestClient externalApiRestClient) {\n        this.restClient = externalApiRestClient;\n    }\n\n    // Use restClient\n}\n```\n\n## Migration Checklist\n\n- [ ] Replace `RestTemplate` field with `RestClient`\n- [ ] Update constructor/configuration to create `RestClient` instance\n- [ ] Replace `.getForEntity()` with `.get().retrieve().body()`\n- [ ] Replace `.postForEntity()` with `.post().body().retrieve().body()`\n- [ ] Replace `.put()` with `.put().body().retrieve()`\n- [ ] Replace `.delete()` with `.delete().retrieve()`\n- [ ] Replace `.exchange()` with fluent API equivalents\n- [ ] Update URI building to use `uriBuilder` or path variables\n- [ ] Update error handling to use `.onStatus()`\n- [ ] Update generic types to use `ParameterizedTypeReference`\n- [ ] Test all API calls thoroughly\n\n## References\n- [Spring RestClient
